# Skills · A · part 32

827 entries, adaptive-code-orchestrator to adce-core. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **adaptive-code-orchestrator**: Adaptively choose single-agent work, parallel reconnaissance, dependency waves, or independent review for reliable repository changes. Use for uncertain, cross-cutting, multi-file, long-running, or high-risk coding work where agent coordination may improve evidence or speed, or when the user asks how much agent coordination to use. When explicitly invoked for a small localized edit, stay single-agent and keep the workflow minimal. ([CanerKocak/clanker-skills](https://github.com/CanerKocak/clanker-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/clanker-skills/skills/adaptive-code-orchestrator) · ★1)
- **adaptive-code-review**: Evidence-qualified code review adapted to the user's decision, project and risks. ([razzant/OuroborosHub](https://github.com/razzant/OuroborosHub/tree/HEAD/skills/adaptive-code-review) · ★10 · 2 copies)
- **adaptive-codex-orchestrator**: Adaptively plan, delegate, integrate, and verify complex software work using Codex's existing native agents, including explicitly selected GPT-6.1 Terra workers. Use when asked to orchestrate agents, parallelize independent work, coordinate a multi-module implementation, investigate competing bug hypotheses, or manage substantial work with dependencies. Keep tiny or tightly coupled tasks direct. This skill supplies coordination policy, not a new agent runtime. ([abtrk95/adaptive-codex-orchestrator](https://github.com/abtrk95/adaptive-codex-orchestrator/tree/HEAD/adaptive-codex-orchestrator))
- **adaptive-codex-router**: Route Codex tasks to an appropriate model, reasoning effort, speed preference, and orchestration pattern using a quality-first lexicographic policy. - **adaptive-coding-trainer**: 当需要进行交互式、自适应编程练习时使用本 skill——例如用户说"出道题练练""讲讲闭包并出个练习""结合我的项目代码出个题""帮我按遗忘曲线复习薄弱点""这道题我卡住了,针对它出几道变体"。本 skill 将知能行考研数学的自适应刷题思路迁移到编程学习:两层粒度(知识点原子 + 模块链路)、三文件练习(示例/模板/测试)、实现级主流方案教学、SM-2 掌握度跟踪、变体题防背题、三场景统一闭环(项目驱动 / 卡点驱动 / 系统化)。与 codequiz、kp-explain 的关键区别在于:以业界主流规范方案为权威,用户已有项目代码仅作"引出话题的线索 / 对照纠偏",绝不作为标准答案。 ([defier08/adaptive-coding-trainer](https://github.com/defier08/adaptive-coding-trainer/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))

- **adapto-project-learn**: Consolidate what the agent has learned while working — distill the dated notes in .adapto/project/learnings.md into the structured brain facets and close items in open-questions.md — so the project brain gets sharper over time. Reviews a diff before writing; local only (no CMS writes). ([adaptocms/adapto-cms-agent-skills](https://github.com/adaptocms/adapto-cms-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/adapto-project-learn) · ★1)
- **adapto-publish**: Take reviewed draft content live — publish Articles, Pages, and collection items (draft → published), or archive them back (published → archived). Discovers drafts, you select, then it publishes under plan-then-apply. Closes the draft-first loop. ([adaptocms/adapto-cms-agent-skills](https://github.com/adaptocms/adapto-cms-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/adapto-publish) · ★1)
- **adapto-scaffold**: Create a new Adapto-ready frontend by wrapping `npx create-adapto-app` — choose a framework (Next/Astro/SvelteKit), scaffold the project (the Adapto read-client is included), and wire up `.env`. Consent-gated — shows the exact command and runs it only after you approve. New projects only. ([adaptocms/adapto-cms-agent-skills](https://github.com/adaptocms/adapto-cms-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/adapto-scaffold) · ★1)
- **adapto-schema-apply**: Apply an approved .adapto/schema-plan.json to the CMS — create Article categories and custom collections (with a two-pass step for references), idempotently, via the adapto CLI. Plan-then-apply; writes content. Pairs with adapto:schema-design. ([adaptocms/adapto-cms-agent-skills](https://github.com/adaptocms/adapto-cms-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/adapto-schema-apply) · ★1)
- **adapto-schema-design**: Propose a content schema from your project context — the custom collections (and Article categories) the site needs, plus an advisory map of which built-in types cover the rest. Writes a reviewable .adapto/schema-plan.json; no CMS writes. Pairs with adapto:schema-apply. ([adaptocms/adapto-cms-agent-skills](https://github.com/adaptocms/adapto-cms-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/adapto-schema-design) · ★1)
- **adapto-seo-wire**: Make stored SEO metadata actually render — a one-time, consent-gated, framework-aware setup that wires a head-render layer (title, meta, OG/Twitter, JSON-LD) reading the _adapto_seo collection, plus generates llms.txt / llms-full.txt from your content inventory. Edits your app templates (never the read-client), only after you approve the exact changes. ([adaptocms/adapto-cms-agent-skills](https://github.com/adaptocms/adapto-cms-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/adapto-seo-wire) · ★1)
- **adapto-translate**: Translate existing Adapto content (Articles, Pages, collection items, Categories, Microcopy) into another enabled language via create-translation. Structural-parity gate blocks broken translations; glossary-aware; single-item and corpus modes. Plan-then-apply; runs at the top model tier. ([adaptocms/adapto-cms-agent-skills](https://github.com/adaptocms/adapto-cms-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/adapto-translate) · ★1)
- **adaptor**: 把研究问题落成简体中文的用研材料——问卷（甄别 / 满意度 / 题材偏好）与访谈大纲（无主持 / 有主持两版）。管的是题型选择、题干与提问话术的写法、追问方向的分层、选项与自填口径、量表区间、题量与时长取舍。当用户说"设计一份问卷""写访谈大纲""出题""改成有主持人版""这道矩阵题在访谈里怎么问""追问该怎么写""题干太书面了"时使用。产出是中文定稿，之后交 sc-to-en 做英文本地化。 ([Hayasui/adaptor](https://github.com/Hayasui/adaptor/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))

- **adare-devvm**: Drive or debug a running ADARE VM interactively — start/resume/stop dev sessions, fire single actions, replay playbooks, drive toward a goal with the vision agent, hand-author playbooks, reset/checkpoint the VM, and debug CV/OCR grounding. Trigger for hands-on 'click through the VM', 'check what's on screen', 'checkpoint before I try this', 'why didn't it find that icon'. Not this → editing a playbook.yml's actions (adare-playbook); step-by-step live-VM driving/debugging (adare-devvm); building a base VM or environment (adare-vm-build). - **adare-overview**: Start here for any ADARE task when it is unclear which ADARE skill applies, or the task spans several steps (e.g. 'build an experiment that checks whether deleting a file in a text editor leaves artifacts on Ubuntu and Windows'). Gives the mental model (project → environment → experiment → playbook → tests → run → compare), routes to the right adare-* skill, and sets the agent rules for driving the adare CLI (machine-readable output, one live VM, never --force without asking). Not this → a single well-scoped step that clearly matches one adare-* skill. ([fkie-cad/adare](https://github.com/fkie-cad/adare/tree/HEAD/skills/adare-overview) · ★1)
